=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def totalNQueens(self, n: int) -> int:
        def addans(board,ans):
            temp=[]
            for row in board:
                for j in range(len(row)):
                    if row[j]=="Q"
                        temp.append(j+1)
            ans.append(temp)
        def solve(col,board,low,upper,lower,ans,n):
            if col==n:
                addans(board,ans)
                return 
            for row in range(n):
                if low[row]==0 and upper[n-1+col-row]==0 and lower[row+col]==0:
                    board[row][col]="Q"
                    low[row]=1
                    upper[n-1+col-row]=1
                    lower[row+col]=1
                    solve(col+1,board,low,upper,lower,ans,n)
                    low[row]=0
                    upper[n-1+col-row]=0
                    lower[row+col]=0
        ans=[]        
        board=[[0]*n for i in range(n)
        low=[0]*n
        upper=[0]*(2*n-1)
        lower=[0]*(2*n-1)
        solve(0,board,low,upper,lower,ans,n)
        return len(ans)

Observe the following faulty python code. It contains one or more bugs.
Fix the code and output only the fixed code.

```python
class Solution:
    def totalNQueens(self, n: int) -> int:
        def addans(board,ans):
            temp=[]
            for row in board:
                for j in range(len(row)):
                    if row[j]=="Q"
                        temp.append(j+1)
            ans.append(temp)
        def solve(col,board,low,upper,lower,ans,n):
            if col==n:
                addans(board,ans)
                return 
            for row in range(n):
                if low[row]==0 and upper[n-1+col-row]==0 and lower[row+col]==0:
                    board[row][col]="Q"
                    low[row]=1
                    upper[n-1+col-row]=1
                    lower[row+col]=1
                    solve(col+1,board,low,upper,lower,ans,n)
                    low[row]=0
                    upper[n-1+col-row]=0
                    lower[row+col]=0
        ans=[]        
        board=[[0]*n for i in range(n)
        low=[0]*n
        upper=[0]*(2*n-1)
        lower=[0]*(2*n-1)
        solve(0,board,low,upper,lower,ans,n)
        return len(ans)
```

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

class Solution:
    def totalNQueens(self, n: int) -> int:
        def addans(board,ans):
            temp=[]
            for row in board:
                for j in range(len(row)):
                    if row[j]=="Q"
                        temp.append(j+1)
            ans.append(temp)
        def solve(col,board,low,upper,lower,ans,n):
            if col==n:
                addans(board,ans)
                return 
            for row in range(n):
                if low[row]==0 and upper[n-1+col-row]==0 and lower[row+col]==0:
                    board[row][col]="Q"
                    low[row]=1
                    upper[n-1+col-row]=1
                    lower[row+col]=1
                    solve(col+1,board,low,upper,lower,ans,n)
                    low[row]=0
                    upper[n-1+col-row]=0
                    lower[row+col]=0
        ans=[]        
        board=[[0]*n for i in range(n)
        low=[0]*n
        upper=[0]*(2*n-1)
        lower=[0]*(2*n-1)
        solve(0,board,low,upper,lower,ans,n)
        return len(ans)

=== RAW ANSWER ===
.

```python
class Solution:
    def totalNQueens(self, n: int) -> int:
        def addans(board, ans):
            temp = []
            for row in board:
                for j in range(len(row)):
                    if row[j] == "Q":
                        temp.append(j + 1)
            ans.append(temp)

        def solve(col, board, low, upper, lower, ans, n):
            if col == n:
                addans(board, ans)
                return
            for row in range(n):
                if low[row] == 0 and upper[n - 1 + col - row] == 0 and lower[row + col] == 0:
                    board[row][col] = "Q"
                    low[row] = 1
                    upper[n - 1 + col - row] = 1
                    lower[row + col] = 1
                    solve(col + 1, board, low, upper, lower, ans, n)
                    board[row][col] = 0
                    low[row] = 0
                    upper[n - 1 + col - row] = 0
                    lower[row + col] = 0

        ans = []
        board = [[0] * n for _ in range(n)]
        low = [0] * n
        upper = [0] * (2 * n - 1)
        lower = [0] * (2 * n - 1)
        solve(0, board, low, upper, lower, ans, n)
        return len(ans)
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
class Solution:
    def totalNQueens(self, n: int) -> int:
        def addans(board, ans):
            temp = []
            for row in board:
                for j in range(len(row)):
                    if row[j] == "Q":
                        temp.append(j + 1)
            ans.append(temp)

        def solve(col, board, low, upper, lower, ans, n):
            if col == n:
                addans(board, ans)
                return
            for row in range(n):
                if low[row] == 0 and upper[n - 1 + col - row] == 0 and lower[row + col] == 0:
                    board[row][col] = "Q"
                    low[row] = 1
                    upper[n - 1 + col - row] = 1
                    lower[row + col] = 1
                    solve(col + 1, board, low, upper, lower, ans, n)
                    board[row][col] = 0
                    low[row] = 0
                    upper[n - 1 + col - row] = 0
                    lower[row + col] = 0

        ans = []
        board = [[0] * n for _ in range(n)]
        low = [0] * n
        upper = [0] * (2 * n - 1)
        lower = [0] * (2 * n - 1)
        solve(0, board, low, upper, lower, ans, n)
        return len(ans)
